Task ID: 1-7
Agent: main
Task: Fix web app "data failed to load" error, consolidate initial sync, fix first-login state machine, add snapshot sequence semantics

Work Log:
- Audited entire monorepo structure (apps/web, apps/api, apps/desktop, packages)
- Discovered root cause of "data failed to load": Next.js API rewrites were removed, causing 404 for API requests when not going through Caddy gateway
- Added API rewrites back in next.config.ts to proxy /api/* and /socket.io/* to port 3003
- Found and fixed buildUrl() double-? bug: paths with existing query params were getting ?XTransformPort appended instead of &XTransformPort
- Found and fixed Bearer token auth issue: Next.js rewrites don't forward Set-Cookie headers, so httpOnly cookie auth doesn't work through the proxy
- Added module-level token cache in api-client.ts (setSessionToken/clearSessionToken) to avoid timing issue with Zustand persist
- Updated login-form.tsx to call setApiClientToken() immediately after successful login
- Updated use-app-store.ts logout to clear cached token
- Audited duplicate initial sync mechanisms: found initial-sync.js (proper) and sync-service.js's initialSync() (conflicting)
- Removed duplicate initialSync() from sync-service.js
- Fixed setAuth() in sync-service.js to not reset sync version or call _syncCycle() after login
- Added concurrency guard to initial-sync.js
- Removed syncService.initialSync() call from index.js
- Fixed first-login decision logic in loading-screen.js (NOT_INITIALIZED is not an error)
- Added sync:initial-required event in sync-service.js when agency not initialized after login
- Added snapshot sequence recording in initial-sync.js (stores initialSyncSnapshotSequence in _sync_meta)
- Added snapshot sequence as starting cursor in sync-service.js (_pullFromCloud checks for snapshotSequence)
- Added race condition check in initial-sync.js (re-checks snapshotSequence after all stages complete)

Stage Summary:
- "Data failed to load" error fixed: API rewrites + Bearer token auth working
- Initial sync consolidated to exactly ONE implementation (initial-sync.js only)
- First-login state machine: NOT_INITIALIZED → trigger initial sync → READY → incremental sync starts
- Snapshot sequence semantics: initial sync records snapshotSequence, incremental sync uses it as starting cursor
- Race condition handling: re-checks snapshotSequence after completion, emits warning if changed
- All agency dashboard API calls returning 200 with proper auth

Task ID: 2
Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: Fix BLASTI Web dev crash — `bun run dev:web` → ChunkLoadError → browser crash; find root cause, fix dev scripts, bound client loops, add regression protection

Work Log:
- Root cause identified: root dev:web used `bun --bun run node_modules/.bin/next dev` — Next.js 16 dev server ran on Bun's JS runtime (unsupported combo). Evidence: start-server process under Bun with 1.4GB RSS runaway + .next/_events NEXT_CLI_SESSION_STOPPED after 459ms
- Fixed root package.json dev:web → `node node_modules/next/dist/bin/next dev -p 3000 -H 127.0.0.1`; added dev:web:api, dev:web:lan, clean:web, test:web:startup
- Fixed apps/web package.json dev → Node runtime + 127.0.0.1, added dev:lan + clean
- next.config.ts: rewrites return [] in NEXT_BUILD_MODE=export (fixes desktop/mobile static export builds); /socket.io rewrite destination re-appends trailing slash (Next 308-normalizes /socket.io/ → /socket.io BEFORE rewrites; engine.io only answers /socket.io/ → realtime could never connect on direct :3000 access)
- use-realtime.tsx: transports websocket-first → polling-first (WS-first hangs against Next dev forever — upgrade TCP accepted, never completed — so polling was never reached); reconnectionAttempts Infinity → 20 on web; one bounded reconnect per sessionToken change
- Deleted src/hooks/use-realtime.ts (dead duplicate shadowed by .tsx, with divergent reconnect configs — tsc checked .ts while bundler ran .tsx)
- use-discovery-service.ts: 3s scan-status poll now stops after 3 consecutive failures (was unbounded if API died mid-scan)
- connection-status.tsx: health checks honor central isApiUnreachable() 30s cooldown
- New src/components/shared/dev-debug-hud.tsx — opt-in diagnostics (?debug=1 / localStorage blasti:debug=1, Alt+Shift+D hides): API reachability, socket status, request counter, route, JS heap; mounted in all 4 page.tsx render branches, inert unless enabled and in production
- New scripts/web-startup-test.mjs + bun run test:web:startup: spawns dev:web, verifies ready/200/all chunks 200+non-empty/chunk byte stability/bounded second load/idle RSS ceiling — 8/8 passed from a clean .next with API NOT running
- start.sh aligned to Node runtime + 127.0.0.1; daemon-start.sh already safe (delegates to fixed apps/web dev script)
- Audited via 2 subagents: all 48 setInterval sites cleared-or-dormant; socket singleton ref-counted with cleanup; NO server-only packages (Prisma/cloud-db/Node builtins) in client bundle; all 31 lazy() targets exist with resolvable deps; api-client retries bounded (3, exp backoff, 4xx no-retry, 30s unreachable cooldown persisted in sessionStorage)
- Verified via agent-browser on http://127.0.0.1:3000 direct: landing renders, owner1 login → agency dashboard live, realtime CONNECTED over polling ("متصل بالخادم"), 0 page errors, 46/46 resources OK, browser heap 58.7MB, server RSS flat (Δ+1MB/40s idle); web-only mode (API down): graceful error states, bounded single retry chains, page stays interactive; request volume finite (91 req first 10s incl. dev assets; steady state stats 3/health 2/socket ~4/notifications 2 per 35s)
- Final stack restarted fresh: web (Node, 127.0.0.1:3000) + API (127.0.0.1:3003); gateway :81 → web 200 confirmed with loopback binding

Stage Summary:
- Root cause: Bun-runtime Next.js dev server (bun --bun) — chunk-serving corruption + dev-server memory runaway; amplified on Windows by 0.0.0.0 binding, WS-first socket hang, unbounded reconnect, uncoordinated pollers
- bun run dev:web is now standalone-safe: Node runtime, exactly ONE server, 127.0.0.1, no API/Electron dependency, graceful when API is down
- Realtime now works on direct dev access (was silently broken: slash-less /socket.io 404 + WS-first hang)
- Regression protection: bun run test:web:startup (8/8) + opt-in dev HUD (?debug=1)
- Full report: /home/z/my-project/WEB_DEV_CRASH_AUDIT.md

Work Log:
- Read worklog 1-7 context; read fully: local-api/sync-service.js (1849 ln), initial-sync.js (1040), index.js sync parts (WAL 576-693, sync routes 5390-5509, markCloudContact 8271), main.js, loading-screen.js (diagnostics flow), cloud-side sync.ts (861), initial-sync.ts (539), sync-helpers.ts (626), offline-sync lib+routes, eccp-reconciler.ts, packages/core sync-registry/sync-serializer, desktop frontend sync store + 4 components, web db/sync.ts + sync-service.ts + offline-layer + use-offline-sync + indicators
- Traced wire protocol end-to-end and found contract mismatches: desktop pull sends `sinceSequence` but cloud pullSchema (sync.ts:108) has only `cursor/lastPulledAt/agencyId/limit` → sinceSequence silently stripped; desktop push reads `pushResponse.accepted/conflicts` (sync-service.js:1290-1293) but cloud returns `{results}` (sync.ts:743) → _lastPushedVersion never advances
- Verified SyncChange is ONLY written by processPushMutation (grep: zero call sites of recordSyncChange/atomicMutation in business routes) → cloud-originated changes (web/kiosk/QR/cron) never appear in pull
- Verified Task 1-7 snapshot-sequence work present (initial-sync.js:928, sync-service.js:800-821) but defeated server-side by cursor stripping + shared server-side SyncCursor
- Confirmed legacy dual-DB: cloud-sync.js marked @deprecated, required by nobody; local-db.js only required by cloud-sync.js; local-api-routes.js unreferenced (all still packaged in electron-builder.yml:18-20)
- Confirmed packages/core sync-registry/sync-serializer ARE imported by cloud (sync.ts:24, initial-sync.ts:31-32, sync-helpers.ts:20) but NOT by desktop sync-service.js (divergent duplicate lists)
- Confirmed desktop ConflictResolutionDialog + sync-indicator.tsx and web OfflineSyncIndicator + lib/sync-service.ts + offline-queue.ts are unmounted/unreferenced (dead UX paths)
- Mapped model coverage: desktop SYNC_TABLES == cloud SYNC_MODELS (19, match); initial-data stages only 13 → 6 synced models never backfilled; AuditLog written locally at 12+ sites but excluded from sync; local schema (packages/db) has syncVersion/syncedAt/AgencyLocalState/DeletedRecord
- Found poison-pill WAL entries: logPendingMutation('update','Reservation',id,...) legacy convention at index.js:6249/6270/6290/6308/6380/6402/6507/6646 used by the exact endpoints the frontend calls (client.ts:277-297) → replay builds invalid URL → 5 failures → 'abandoned' silently
- Checked auth/offline policy: 3-day OFFLINE_TOKEN_MAX_MS (index.js:99), 401 in incremental sync only hits generic backoff (no re-auth event); initial-sync handles AUTH_FAILURE properly
Stage Summary:
- P0: cloud pull advances a SHARED per-agency SyncCursor on send (sync.ts:252-253) and ignores the desktop's sinceSequence (zod strips it) → crash/multi-client data loss; Task 1-7 baseline ineffective end-to-end
- P0: no cloud business route records SyncChange → pull only ever returns echo of desktop's own pushes → desktop never receives cloud-side changes
- P0: push response contract mismatch → _lastPushedVersion never advances → entire agency dataset re-pushed every 2-min cycle, SyncChange/syncVersion churn unbounded
- P1: poison-pill WAL mutations from queue-op endpoints ('update'/'Model' convention) fail 5× then abandoned silently = data loss; partial mitigation only via diff push
- P1: LOW_FREQUENCY model filter skips changes but cursor advances past them; legacy-path tombstones (seq 0) block re-creation of resurrected records for 30 days
- P1: initial-sync _transformRecord inflates local syncVersion=cloud+1 → every incremental update misclassified as conflict → _sync_conflicts grows unbounded, resolution UI not wired
- Coverage gaps: SmsSettings, PaymentSettings, GlobalAnnouncement, PlanFeature, Favorite, FAQ absent from initial-data stages (never imported fresh); AuditLog written locally but never synced
- Dead code: cloud-sync.js, local-db.js, local-api-routes.js (still packaged), web lib/sync-service.ts, web offline-queue.ts, desktop sync-indicator.tsx, both ConflictResolutionDialogs unmounted
- Web has a 2nd WatermelonDB sync engine (db/sync.ts via db/provider.tsx) sharing the same cloud endpoints → cursor contention with desktop; pull response lacks `timestamp` → WDB incremental broken
- UX: InitialSync.tsx granular + resumable (good); sync UI reads stale/wrong field names (sync-status-detail vs /api/sync/status stale module vars); no manual-sync path fires on connectivity restore (process.on('online') dead, preload events unused)

Stage Summary:
- BLASTI = Arabic-first multi-tenant queue-management SaaS for Algerian institutions. Platforms: apps/web (Next.js 16/React 19, :3000, hash-SPA), apps/api (Hono on Bun + Socket.IO, :3003), apps/desktop (Electron local-first: Vite frontend + local Hono API :3080 + own SQLite, offline 3-day tokens), apps/mobile (Capacitor 6 Android/iOS shell), packages/db (shared Prisma+SQLite at packages/db/data/custom.db), packages/core (sync registry SSOT); reverse proxy gateway on :81 (XTransformPort routing)
- Auth: custom JWT HS256 (jose, NEXTAUTH_SECRET, 30-day expiry), NextAuth-compatible cookie on web / Authorization Bearer on native; roles SUPER_ADMIN / AGENCY_OWNER / STAFF / CUSTOMER; guard hierarchy requireAuth, requireAdmin, requireRole, requireAgencyAccess, requireStaffPermission (live DB check, not JWT claims), requireResourceOwnership; stale-JWT rejection when iat < User.lastRoleChangeAt
- Data flow: browser -> Next.js rewrites (/api/* + /socket.io/* -> localhost:3003) -> Hono routes -> Prisma -> SQLite; realtime = API routes POST /emit + /emit-batch (x-internal-secret) -> Socket.IO rooms agency:<id> / customer:<id> / kiosk:<id> / admin:global; device heartbeat pipeline (30s heartbeat, 90s offline, REBOOT/REFRESH/CONFIG_UPDATE commands)
- ~42-table schema: User, Agency, Branch, Counter (no direct agencyId), Service, Reservation, AgencyStaff (8 granular permissions), AgencyDevice (TV/KIOSK/DISPLAY/PRINTER + pairingCode), Review, Notification, Transaction (version field), SubscriptionPlan, SmsLog/SmsPurchase/SmsSettings, PaymentSettings, DelayedJob, FAQ, Favorite, GlobalAnnouncement, PlanFeature, AuditLog, DeletedRecord tombstones, SyncChange
- Notification router (cost cascade): WebSocket (0 DZD) -> FCM push (0 DZD) -> carrier SMS/WhatsApp (paid, last resort); ADVANCE_WARNING scheduled via DelayedJob with 25% mathematical buffer; TURN_CALL dispatches immediately; worker polls DelayedJob every 30s (batch 50); WhatsApp unimplemented -> SMS fallback; balance deduction agency.sponsorSms ? agency.smsBalance : user.freeSmsCount
- Docs-marked legacy/superseded: storageProvider+storageKey pattern replaces legacy avatarUrl/logoUrl fields (kept for backward compat); desktop local-api-routes.js + local-db.js + cloud-sync.js = dead dual-DB sync architecture (worklog 4-a says deleted - ALL THREE STILL PRESENT in this clone); hand-copied desktop SYNC_TABLES superseded by packages/core/sync-registry.json SSOT; port-3010 device-discovery mini-service + /api/probe?XTransformPort=3010 superseded by embedded /api/agency-devices/discovery/* handlers + POST /api/agency-devices/discovery/probe
- Worklog-vs-tree drift (KEY FINDING): the current clone does NOT contain the worklog's fixes - apps/web/next.config.ts rewrites are REMOVED (comment says so at line 31), no scripts/dev-api.cjs or scripts/dev-web.cjs exist, packages/cloud-db is missing, apps/desktop/frontend is missing, deprecated desktop files still present; worklog Task 2 also flagged shadow-file bugs (.ts vs .tsx use-realtime) and duplicate initial-sync implementations since consolidated

Work Log:
- VERIFIED TREE STATE (differs from earlier worklog entries — earlier fix waves lived on a tree state not present in this clone): cloud sync-helpers.ts + initial-sync.ts import missing @blasti/cloud-db and are UNMOUNTED; desktop sync-service.js is the OLD polling generation (7 tables, 120s interval, timestamp cursors, push reads wrong response fields → _lastPushedVersion never advances, two competing outbound paths); desktop initial-sync.js (proper state machine) has ZERO consumers; loading-screen.js has a hand-rolled incomplete import (Agency/Services/Branches/Counters/Staff, hardcoded city); desktop frontend api-client.ts getApiBaseUrl() is CLOUD-FIRST (BLASTI_CLOUD_URL||localhost:3003, :3080 only as LAN failover) violating local-first; packages/db schema lacks SyncChange/SyncMutation/AgencyLocalState (initial-sync.js needs agencyLocalState delegate → would crash); packages/core exports map lacks ./sync-registry + ./sync-serializer subpaths used by apps/api; cloud business routes record NO SyncChange (change feed basis missing); socket.io-client missing from desktop deps
- DEFINED TARGET CONTRACTS (v2 sync protocol):
  * Pull: POST /api/sync/pull {agencyId?, sinceSequence?, limit?} → {success, timestamp, latestSequence, hasMore, changes:{[Model]:{changed:[records], deleted:[ids]}}} — SyncChange-table-driven, per-agency, paginated, tombstones included
  * Push: outbox (WAL) replay to cloud business routes with X-Idempotency-Key header; cloud route-agnostic Hono middleware records/checks SyncMutation (2xx-only) → duplicate replays return stored response; periodic reconciliation record-diff push via POST /api/sync/push {mutations:[{mutationId,model,recordId,operation,data,expectedVersion?}]} → {results:[{mutationId,status,conflict?}]}
  * Real-time: cloud middleware auto-records SyncChange for 19 synced models → debounced notifier emits sync:changes {agencyId, latestSequence, models} to Socket.IO room agency:<id>; desktop main-process socket.io-client triggers immediate debounced incremental pull; fallback 30s incremental pull + 15-min full reconciliation; local API :3080 gains Socket.IO server emitting UI event names (queue:called etc.) on local mutations + post-pull so UI refreshes in seconds offline and online
  * Initial sync: initial-sync.js (state machine NOT_INITIALIZED→INITIALIZING→READY/FAILED in AgencyLocalState) becomes THE authoritative initializer driven by loading-screen with per-stage progress; launch gated on GET /api/db-status readiness; fresh install offline = blocked with clear message
  * Frontend: getApiBaseUrl() Electron → http://127.0.0.1:3080 ALWAYS (cloud = sync only); local login proxies unknown users to cloud and upserts locally for future offline login
- STARTED FOUNDATION (this entry, in progress): schema additions, core exports, sync-helpers v2, tracking middleware, sync.ts v2, initial-data mount

Stage Summary:
- Audit complete; contracts fixed for all downstream tasks; foundation implementation started

  * `logPendingMutation` inserted Date.now() into INTEGER `created_at` → quaint binds INTEGER as Int32 → every outbox insert would fail ("does not fit in an INT column"). Rebuilt `_pending_mutations` with BIGINT created_at/last_attempt_at + one-time `_migratePendingMutationsBigint()` table rebuild (rows preserved, introspects old shape before copy)
  * Same BIGINT fix for `_sync_applied_mutations.appliedAt`

- FINAL: all 3 desktop suites PASS (Database Layer, Sync Engine, Local API Integration — 31 passed, 0 failed, 19 auth-gated skips)
- CRITICAL production bug found via E2E browser verification: EVERY business mutation 500'd. Root cause (two compounding):
  1. sync-helpers auto-tracking hook ran awaited (resolveAgencyForRecord queries + SyncChange insert via dbRaw) INSIDE business interactive $transactions → SQLite write contention → "Transaction already closed" (5s tx timeout) → 500
  2. sequence allocation was read-then-write (SELECT MAX+1 then create) → P2002 "Unique constraint failed on (sequence)" under concurrency
  FIX: hook is now FIRE-AND-FORGET (enqueueSyncEvent → serialized FIFO background worker; business mutations never wait on sync bookkeeping; ordering preserved; crash-loss healed by full reconciliation per spec) + sequence allocated ATOMICALLY in single INSERT..SELECT COALESCE(MAX)+1 statement (with RETURNING) in BOTH recordChangeAuto and tx-based recordSyncChange — collision impossible under SQLite single-writer
- Verified: CALL NEXT → 200 (was 500), SyncChange rows recorded, 651 rows / ZERO duplicate sequences, POST /api/sync/pull cursor feed returns changed records per model with latestSequence
- Web E2E via agent-browser through gateway :81 (correct user path; raw :3000 has no proxy by design — rewrites removed to fix Next.js 16 crash): owner1 → agency dashboard live data (queue entries, ETA, stats, quick actions); admin → dashboard (+12%/+15%/+22%/99.9%, agencies mgmt, announcements); customer1 → home (agencies, activity, badges) at desktop + 390px mobile (RTL intact, bottom nav)
- Fixed health-check false-negative: gateway only forwards /api/* (root /health bare 404 lacks CORS → fetch throws → phantom "CLOUD FAIL" + offline banner). Added /api/health alias on cloud API + switched connection-status.tsx to it → CLOUD OK, banner gone
- UI golden path via browser: CALL NEXT button → C-006 called → إتمام (Complete) → Reservation.status=COMPLETED with calledAt/completedAt in DB
- Lint: apps/web 0 problems (public/** minified prebuilt bundles added to eslint ignores — they were the only 39 errors; removed one stale eslint-disable in device-kiosk.tsx). apps/api tsc: 1 sync-scope error fixed (offline-sync.ts c.json status cast); remaining ~217 are pre-existing domain debt (admin/agency-devices/sms etc., untouched by redesign, runtime-safe under bun)

- Shared schema (packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma): User.passwordHash String → String? (cloud-only secret; synced desktop profiles NULL) + new LocalDeviceCredential model (userId, deviceId, verifierHash, salt, algo=scrypt, scryptN/R/P, revokedAt, @@unique([userId,deviceId])) + User backrelation. DDL regenerated via the documented `prisma migrate diff --from-empty --to-schema-datamodel` pipeline (verified byte-identical reproduction of the pre-change file FIRST, then regenerated → 47 tables). Prisma client regenerated (LocalDeviceCredential delegate + nullable hash confirmed).

Two swap-by-rename strategies were implemented and empirically DISPROVEN live (SQLite ≥3.25 always repoints child FK clauses on RENAME even with legacy_alter_table=ON; dropping a referenced parent leaves an unresolvable deferred RESTRICT violation → "Foreign key constraint violated" at COMMIT). Final proven approach: in-place column surgery — ADD nullable column → UPDATE copy → DROP COLUMN (guarded: no views/triggers on User, passwordHash not indexed, SQLite ≥3.35) → RENAME COLUMN. Parent table never renamed/dropped → zero FK machinery. Verified on simulated legacy DBs (unstampeded adoption path + stamped-v1 path + idempotent re-run): nullability relaxed, seeded user + FK-child Agency rows intact, unique constraints intact, foreign_key_check + integrity_check clean, and THE ORIGINAL KILLER (Prisma upsert of a cloud User without passwordHash) now succeeds.
- initial-sync.js: MODEL_SYNC_EXCLUDED_FIELDS applied in _transformRecord (single choke point for Prisma upsert + raw fallback + deferred replay) — auth secrets can never persist locally even if a cloud build regresses. sync-service.js: same exclusion in _cloudRecordToLocal for the incremental pull path. Generic for ALL roles.
- Local auth redesigned (local-api/index.js): stable per-installation deviceId persisted in _sync_meta; scrypt (N=16384,r=8,p=1,keylen=64) verifier derivation + timing-safe verification; POST /api/auth/login resolution order: (1) LOCAL UNLOCK via LocalDeviceCredential (fully offline), (2) legacy local passwordHash for pre-contract rows (mismatch falls THROUGH to the cloud — stale local hash never blocks a changed password), (3) CLOUD LOGIN PROXY → upserts profile-only local User + imports cloud session + stores THIS user's device credential, (4) explicit NO_LOCAL_CREDENTIAL/cloudLoginRequired verdict when cloud is unreachable and no credential exists. New auth'd endpoints POST /api/auth/device-credential (accepts password or pre-derived verifier+salt; optional keepSignedIn persisted to _sync_meta) and DELETE /api/auth/device-credential (revoke; logout alone does NOT revoke). GET /api/auth/session now reports keepSignedIn (default true — historic behavior). Stale-client guard: db.localDeviceCredential absence degrades gracefully with a regenerate-client hint.
- loading-screen.js: keep-signed-in gate in step 0 — an explicit _sync_meta keep_signed_in='false' suppresses auto-restore of blasti-auth.json (password unlock required); default/unreadable DB keeps the always-restore behavior. (Full enforcement of the renderer's own auto-restore needs a frontend change — deliberately NOT touched.)
- Cloud identity: hardcoded version strings 0.2.0 → 0.2.1 in apps/api/src/index.ts + package.json bump so the desktop probe confirms the new build. Live-verified: /api/health → version 0.2.1, bootId, sync.registered=true with 4 routes; POST /api/sync/pull unauth → 401.

Stage Summary:
- The User sync/auth contract now matches the security architecture: cloud User keeps its auth secret; the wire carries an explicit profile-only allow-list; local synced profiles store passwordHash=NULL; desktop unlock is owned by LocalDeviceCredential scoped to (user, device); keep-signed-in controls auto-restore. The Users initial-sync stage failure ("Argument passwordHash is missing") is structurally eliminated for fresh and existing databases.

- ROOT CAUSE of all three lying diagnostics (cloud 404 + import-session 404 + "Queue created: id=undefined"): the Task 14 fix assigned `request.method = 'POST'` AFTER construction — Electron's ClientRequest.method is NOT a writable instance property, the assignment is silently ignored, every "POST" probe still went out as GET. Proven by round-6 logs: server logged "no route matched: GET /api/auth/import-session" while diagnostics printed POST; cloud identity was v0.2.1 (new build) yet the probe 404'd while the engine's real POST pulled pages fine; queue-CRUD "create" actually executed the GET list handler → `{success:true, data:[...]}` → data.id undefined → stale "no session" placeholder survived as the verdict.
- loading-screen.js: all five helpers (postUrl/postAuthUrl/putUrl/patchUrl/deleteUrl) now pass the method in the OPTIONS OBJECT at construction — `net.request({ method, url })` (documented constructor path); deleteUrl's in-try assignment removed. Remaining string-form net.request sites are intentional GETs (probeUrl, fetchWithAuth, main.js URL probes).
- Launch-readiness separation (finalizeDiagnostics): three-level model implemented — FATAL (local DB/schema/workspace-not-READY when it must be) stays error; RECOVERABLE (cloud-api/reconnect-cloud/disconnect-cloud CONNECTIVITY errors) are DOWNGRADED to warning when the workspace is verified READY (verify step readiness.ready === true), with an explicit bilingual note + console line ("cloud unavailability degrades synchronization, not the desktop"); DIAGNOSTIC failures report truthfully without gating. Fresh installs keep strict blocking (NOT verified READY → connectivity errors stay FATAL). main.js computes hasErrors from the downgraded array → READY + cloud-down now auto-launches offline-first.
- Queue-CRUD test rewritten: no pre-assigned verdict (final guard errors on a missing verdict — the stale-placeholder bug is structurally impossible); create asserts success === true AND string id AND ticket (displayNumber|queueNumber|ticketNumber) else ERROR ("contract violation" — never reports a creation without an id); verify step is advisory with SQLite as authoritative; cancel switched from the ineffective PUT /api/reservations/:id {status} (400 — status not in allowedFields, silently "reachable→OK") to the real transactional POST /api/queue/cancel/:id with success assertion; final verdict from SQLite status === 'CANCELLED'; walk-in fields (walkInCustomerName/isWalkIn) replace non-schema customerName.
- CURSOR INVARIANT (the 890→893 jump, root-caused): engine _getCursor() prefers the v2 key `lastPulledSequence` and only falls back to `_lastPulledSequence` when it is NULL — the initial-sync bridge wrote ONLY the legacy key, so a stale over-advanced v2 value (893, left by an older build's pull) shadowed the bridge's correct final (890) forever; the coordinator path (ensureWorkspaceInitialized) adopted NO cursor at all, and the other two call sites adopted raw snapshotSequence (S) instead of the bridge final (F). Fixes: (a) _getCursor dual-key divergence guard — keys disagree → adopt MIN, heal both keys, loud warning (re-apply is ledger-idempotent, skipping forward is not); (b) _setCursor logs REWINDs explicitly; (c) new adoptInitialSyncCursor(result, source) — prefers bridgeFinalSequence ≥ snapshotSequence, writes a lastCursorAdoption audit row (from/to/source/S/F/at), logs the full S → F → cursor receipt; (d) initial-sync.js Step 4e — BOTH cursor keys written AND engine adoption BEFORE the READY flip (the Part D gate unblocks pulls the instant READY is set; the old code wrote the cursor after READY); bridge log now states the invariant receipt; (e) coordinator + main.js IPC + local-api HTTP paths all adopt through the new helper; runInitialSync return + SYNC_COMPLETED now carry bridgeFinalSequence.
- Proof test (user's exact scenario, real engine + real SQLite + mock cloud HTTP server, in the existing test-sync.js harness): seeds the field divergence (v2=893/legacy=890) → hydration resolves 890 via the guard → first pull requests sinceSequence 890 (asserted from the mock's request log) → applies the three SubscriptionPlan changes (891, 892, 893) EXACTLY ONCE (SQLite COUNT = 3) → final cursor 893 with BOTH keys agreeing → second cycle applies 0, no duplication, cursor stable at 893. Plus: adoption audit row assertions and the re-import rewind case (stale 893 → bridge final 890). Suite: 19 passed / 0 failed (was 16); full desktop runner green (Database, Schema Lifecycle, Sync Engine, Local API Integration).
- Cloud /health verified truthful already (introspects app.routes at boot — sync { registered, routes[], protocolVersion } + bootId); no cloud changes needed this round; the 404 was 100% the client's wire method. Self-identifying 404 + fixHint classification retained for genuinely split/stale origins.

Stage Summary:
- One ineffective property assignment was the single root cause of all three "route inconsistencies" and the phantom CRUD success — fixed at the documented Electron API level; diagnostics now probe with real POSTs (import-session over HTTP becomes the normal path again; the in-process fallback is a true safety net).

- Windows next-run expectations: cloud probe shows sync endpoint 200/401 (never a false 404); "Session imported to local API" over HTTP; queue CRUD prints real id + ticket and ends SUCCESS (or an honest ERROR with the phase); "Cursor invariant: snapshot=X → bridge final=Y → engine cursor=Z" after initial sync; READY + cloud down → "DOWNGRADED to warning" line and the dashboard launches in offline mode.

- Created apps/desktop/local-api/local-realtime.js: Socket.IO server attached to the local API HTTP server. Mirrors the cloud protocol exactly (join/leave room, agency, customer, kiosk, admin + an `auth` upgrade event; payload shape { type, agencyId, userId, data, timestamp }; broadcast routing copied from apps/api broadcastEvent). Security: loopback-only, handshake token timing-safe-compared to the CURRENT local session token; unauthenticated sockets may connect (kiosk displays) but can only join kiosk rooms; room joins are authorized against the session user (own agency / own user id / SUPER_ADMIN).
- Wired init into index.js startLocalApi (after serve()) + bridged the in-process emitEvent stream onto the socket server (every local business mutation now reaches the UI live, OFFLINE included); closeLocalRealtime in stopLocalApi; exported broadcast/relay/stats from index.js.
- Added cloud→local realtime relay in sync-service.js _setupSocket: socket.onAny re-emits cloud agency-room events into the local agency room via relayCloudRealtime (skips internal 'sync:changes'); relay context follows the engine's agency.
- Fixed outbox replay data-loss (the user log's PERMANENT FAILED rows): cloud POST /api/reservations is the CUSTOMER-only join-queue route (403 for AGENCY_OWNER) and its Zod schema requires agencyId (local route derives it from the session), so locally-issued tickets replayed as HTTP rows could NEVER reach the cloud. Converted all local record-CREATE outbox rows to canonical SYNC_PUSH creates (logDeterministicOutcome) which are id-preserving, idempotent, role-agnostic: /api/reservations, /api/agency/queue/walk-in, /api/services, /api/agency/services, /api/agency/branches, /api/agency/counters. Also added a replay-time agencyId injection shim for LEGACY POST /api/reservations rows (idempotency key derived from the raw body BEFORE injection so dedup stays stable).
- READY-gate truthful diagnostics (the unexplained "Pull/replay BLOCKED - AgencyLocalState is not READY" after cloud shutdown in the user log): _isAgencyReady now logs exactly WHAT it saw — "NO AgencyLocalState row for agency X" vs "initializationStatus=STATE (expected READY)" vs the query error — rate-limited to once per 60s. Next run will pinpoint the gate regression root cause; no behavior weakened (the gate still strictly blocks).
- Fixed the Electron health-check mislabel in connection-status.tsx: getApiBaseUrl() returns the LOCAL API in Electron, so "cloudReachable" actually probed :3080. Added resolveCloudBaseUrl() which reads the real cloud origin from the main process (electronAPI.getSyncStatus() → syncService.getStatus().cloudBaseUrl; dev fallback http://localhost:3003). Banner now correctly shows online / "local mode — cloud sync paused" / offline per the post-READY severity model.
- Added the global renderer error reporter (apps/web/src/lib/error-reporter.ts, self-installing): window error + unhandledrejection → console + IPC forward; ErrorBoundary.componentDidCatch forwards caught React errors with component stack. New preload bridge reportRendererError + main-process 'renderer:error' handler print `[RendererError]` with FULL stack into the `bun run electron:dev` terminal — this is the instrumentation that will pinpoint the reported "Cannot read properties of null (reading 'split')" (no .split call sites exist anywhere in the desktop main/local-api/sync/loading code; the crash is renderer-side and will surface with its stack next run).
- Verification: node --check on all edited desktop files; module smoke tests (exports, lifecycle, no-op broadcasts); full socket round-trip test (bad token → unauthenticated + join rejected; valid token → authenticated; join:customer(other) rejected; agency room delivery; cloud-shaped payload; auth-event upgrade); Hono+Socket.IO coexistence test on the real @hono/node-server (HTTP /api/health 200 AND socket event delivery on the same server); tsc --noEmit + eslint clean for the edited web files (371 pre-existing app-wide TS errors untouched).

Stage Summary:
- Desktop realtime is now genuinely local-first: UI socket connects to the local API, works fully offline, and receives cloud-originated events via the engine relay while online. This fixes the "offline mode even when cloud is available" report at its root.
- Locally-created records (tickets, services, branches, counters, walk-ins) now reach the cloud exactly once with their LOCAL ids via the canonical push protocol — the PERMANENT FAILED 400/403 outbox rows stop accumulating.
- The READY gate and the health banner now tell the truth with actionable detail; the null .split crash will surface with a full stack via [RendererError] on the next run.

Task: Round 13 — (a) fix "invalid response from the server" when uploading a profile image at account creation; (b) add preset profile icons for users who don't want to upload a photo; (c) redesign the register UI (phone-styled) into a responsive layout for the desktop app and PC web that adapts between phone and desktop sizes.

Work Log:
- ROOT CAUSE of the upload bug (three stacked failures): apps/web/src/hooks/use-upload.ts uploaded via a RAW XMLHttpRequest to the RELATIVE url /api/upload?type=… — (1) the Next.js web app has NO /api/upload route (uploads live on the cloud API and apiClient exists precisely to resolve absolute base URLs) so Next.js returned an HTML 404 page and JSON.parse threw the literal "Invalid response from server"; (2) Electron loads the bundled static export from file:// so a relative XHR has no server at all; (3) even the cloud API's POST /api/upload was a PLACEHOLDER that stored nothing and returned no url.
- Cloud API (apps/api/src/routes/upload.ts rewritten): real disk storage under <cwd>/uploads/<type>/ with unguessable filenames (Date.now()-uuid8.ext); POST returns {success,url: ABSOLUTE(origin)/api/upload/file/<type>/<name>,filename,provider:'local',size}; NEW public GET /api/upload/file/:type/:name serves bytes with correct Content-Type + immutable cache (unauthenticated by design — <img> tags can't send Authorization; filenames are capability links); DELETE now understands the new URL shape (legacy /uploads/* best-effort kept). AUTH SPLIT: avatar uploads are PUBLIC-but-restricted (registration uploads an avatar BEFORE the account exists — Task 22 issues the session only after OTP verification; old requireAuth-first design would 401 exactly where the user reported the bug): per-IP rate limit (10/min, abuse-block wired), images-only whitelist (no SVG/PDF on the public path), 2MB cap; every other type (logo/receipt/general) stays requireAuth + full whitelist + 5MB.
- use-upload.ts rewritten on apiFetch → apiClient: correct ABSOLUTE base url on every platform (web → cloud API, Electron → local API with the new proxy, Capacitor → cloud), keeps failover/retry, FormData passed untouched (no manual Content-Type), AbortController wired into reset(), errors now surface the server's JSON message (no more JSON.parse crashes on HTML).
- Desktop local-api (index.js): POST /api/upload proxy forwards the multipart body byte-exact (arrayBuffer + verbatim Content-Type with boundary) to the cloud, attaching the cloud session token when one exists — deliberately NOT session-gated (register-time uploads have no session yet; the cloud enforces its own per-type policy); DELETE /api/upload proxy is requireAuth-gated; offline → explicit 503 CLOUD_UNREACHABLE (same contract as the register/verify proxies).
- Preset profile icons (register-form.tsx): 12 brand-styled avatars built as compact SVG data URIs (vector glyphs — person, briefcase, star, heart, bolt, sprout, smiley, football, camera, crown, music, shield — over emerald/teal/amber/rose/orange/lime/cyan/green/violet/fuchsia/red/slate gradients, gradient cutout trick via fill="url(#g)"). Chosen because data URIs: pass z.string().url() (verified against the installed zod v4), render in EVERY existing <img avatarUrl> across web/desktop/mobile with zero component changes, sync as plain strings, work offline, and keep the session JWT at ~851 chars. UI: "Profile photo (optional)" panel — upload button + "or pick a ready-made icon" wrap-grid of 12; picking a preset clears an in-flight/finished upload and vice-versa; X removes both; the picked data URI is sent as avatarUrl at register.

All Task 22 logic (required email, verification gate, session bootstrap) and Task 20 desktop agency-only behavior preserved verbatim.
- BONUS BUG FIXED (found while browser-testing): useUsernameAvailability treated ANY apiFetch failure as "taken" — apiFetch never throws, so a 404/unreachable API returned a body without `available` and setStatus('taken') fired, wrongly blocking registration on every API hiccup. Now only a real 200 with a boolean decides availability; failures map to 'error' (non-blocking).
- Verification: eslint clean; tsc 305 pre-existing errors, ZERO new (web+api); node --check local-api OK. curl against the restarted cloud API: public avatar upload 201+absolute URL → GET byte-identical (image/png, immutable cache) → DELETE 401 unauthenticated → receipt upload 401 → traversal probe 400 → file on disk under apps/api/uploads/avatar/. Real register with preset data-URI avatar → 201 requiresVerification → POST /auth/verify with dev codes (1234/12345678) → verified:true + session, user.avatarUrl = preset, JWT 851 chars with avatarUrl claim. Local-api integration test (stub cloud :3997 + real local API :3081, fresh SQLite): 8/8 PASS — cloud-backed login session, upload forwarded (cloud Authorization header, multipart boundary preserved, body intact, 201+url), DELETE forwarded + 401 without session, offline → 503 CLOUD_UNREACHABLE. agent-browser E2E: web 1440px two-panel (brand panel visible, username/email on one row, 430px card), 12 presets render, preset click → data-URI preview + selection ring that SURVIVES step navigation; phone 390px single card (brand hidden, stacked full-width fields, wrapped presets, 55vh scroll active); electron override → agency-only role + agency brand voice; real file-change event → apiFetch POST with FormData → preview switches to the uploaded URL, preset deselected, zero page errors; register submit captured (fetch patch) with avatarUrl = preset data URI (562 chars) and the OTP verification view rendering (2 OTP inputs, masked targets). Screenshots in /home/z/task23-tests/. Cloud API restarted to load the new route (subshell+disown needed for sandbox persistence). Probe accounts left in dev cloud DB: probe.preset.avatar, probe23avatarfull (verified), ui23web10968 (browser run was fetch-stubbed, no real account).

Work Log:
- ROOT CAUSE found (different bug from Task 23's): the cloud route apps/api/src/routes/upload.ts read the upload `type` ONLY from the multipart FORM field (`formData.get('type')`), while EVERY client sends the type ONLY as the `?type=` QUERY param (use-upload.ts hook, customer-settings.tsx, agency-profile.tsx). The form field was therefore ALWAYS absent → rawType defaulted to 'general' → the requireAuth() branch fired even on the public registration-avatar path → exactly the 401 "Authentication required" the desktop register flow reported. Task 23's curl verification had passed only because it manually included `-F type=avatar`; the real clients never did.
- Cloud fix (upload.ts): type resolution is now form field FIRST (authoritative when present), then `c.req.query('type')`, then 'general'. One-line semantic fix that repairs every client at once.
- Belt-and-braces client fixes (protect against older cloud builds that read only the form field): use-upload.ts now appends `type` to the FormData alongside the query param; customer-settings.tsx (profile-image update) appends `type=avatar`; agency-profile.tsx (agency-logo upload) appends `type=logo`. Receipt uploads go through useUpload(type='receipt') and were fixed by the hook change.
- FULL upload-site audit of apps/web: register avatar (useUpload/avatar), customer profile image (raw apiFetch/avatar), agency logo (raw apiFetch/logo), subscription payment receipt ×2 (agency-subscription.tsx + subscription/payment-form.tsx, useUpload/receipt), admin app-binary upload (admin-app-settings.tsx → POST /api/app-versions/upload — that route already reads platform/version from the form fields the client sends, so it only had a routing gap, see next).
- Desktop local-api (index.js): added an `/api/app-versions/*` + `/api/app-versions` cloud passthrough (all methods, multipart bodies forwarded byte-exact, cloud session token attached when a session exists, JSON and binary responses pass through, cloud-down → 503 CLOUD_UNREACHABLE). Previously the admin panel's app-version upload/list inside the desktop shell hit a bare local 404 because app-versions existed only on the cloud. Authorization stays cloud-enforced (requireAdmin).
- People avatars (register-form.tsx): replaced the Task 23 object-glyph presets (briefcase/star/heart/bolt/camera/…) with 12 illustrated PEOPLE busts as compact SVG data URIs — bearded man, long-hair woman, curly-hair man with glasses, hair-bun woman, spiky-hair boy, pigtails girl, bald man with full beard, hijab woman, elder man with grey hair + glasses, bob-cut woman, afro man, long-hair woman with golden clip — over the same 12-color gradient palette (no blues/indigos), four skin tones, shared friendly face (eyes + smile), composable PersonSpec builder (back hair / fringe / extras layers). Still data URIs: pass z.string().url(), render in every existing <img>, sync as plain strings, JWT-safe. UI copy updated in ar+en ("أو اختر صورة شخصية جاهزة:" / "Or pick a ready-made avatar:").

Email stays 8-digit/12345678.
- Agency "Not found" (root cause): CreateAgencyForm POSTs /api/agencies, which existed ONLY on the cloud; the local API had no route → its self-identifying 404 {"error":"Not found"}. Fixed with a POST/GET/PUT/PATCH /api/agencies cloud proxy in local-api/index.js (session token attached, XTransformPort stripped, 503 CLOUD_UNREACHABLE with a clear message when offline; on success it kicks both the record sync and file-sync so the agency appears locally immediately).
- Working days: new `Agency.workingDays` (CSV of weekday numbers 0=Sun…6=Sat, default "1,2,3,4,5") in the shared Prisma schema + local schema-init DDL + local migration **v4** (guarded ALTER; version bumped 3→4; FileAsset added to required tables so the generic convergence top-up also self-heals every DB shape). Cloud: agencies POST persists it, PATCH /api/agency/working-hours accepts it, GET /api/agency/profile + GET /api/agencies return it. Local: PATCH /api/agency/working-hours + GET/PUT /api/agency/profile handle it (outbox-logged). UI: the create form's hours step now shows a localized 7-day toggle chip row BESIDE the time inputs (Intl weekday names, ar/fr/en; Algeria Sun–Thu preselected); preview shows the days; agency-profile.tsx shows a days row under the hours.
- Services: the create form gained a dedicated Services STEP (5 steps now) — dynamic rows with a single-letter queue prefix (auto A, B, C… collision-aware) + name, add/remove. Services are submitted INSIDE the agency create body and created ATOMICALLY server-side (nested create in POST /api/agencies, prefixes auto-assigned, each created Service explicitly captured for the sync feed per spec Part O, mirroring the QueueSettings capture). zod schema `services` array (≤20) in adminCreateAgencySchema.
- FILING SYSTEM AUDIT + ORGANIZATION (cloud): new lib/storage.ts is the single owner of storage decisions — STORAGE_ROOT (env BLASTI_STORAGE_DIR, default <apps/api>/uploads), organized layout `<bucket>/<yyyy>/<mm>/<machine-name>` (avatar|logo|receipt|document|general), unguessable filenames, traversal-hardened resolution with LEGACY flat-layout fallback on every read, URL building (env BLASTI_PUBLIC_BASE_URL override), URL→storagePath parsing. upload.ts rewritten on top of it, now also registers every upload in the new **FileAsset** cloud table (idempotency key deviceFileId, checksum sha256, owner/agency attribution) and returns BOTH absolute url and relative path. app-versions binaries moved OUT of os.tmpdir() (wiped on reboot!) into <STORAGE_ROOT>/app-versions with a read-fallback to the old tmpdir location.
- DESKTOP LOCAL FILE STORAGE + FILE SYNC (the headline feature): new local-api/lib/file-store.js (BLASTI_LOCAL_FILES_DIR, default <userData>/blasti-files — main.js sets it beside the DB dir; same bucket/yyyy/mm layout, traversal guards, status diagnostics) and local-api/lib/file-sync.js — a worker that mirrors the local store with the cloud: PUSH local-only/dirty files → POST /api/files/sync/push (multipart + meta, idempotent by deviceFileId), PULL cloud files since a `_sync_meta` cursor → GET /api/files/sync/pull + /blob/:id → saved locally + rows inserted SYNCED, TOMBSTONES replayed via DELETE /api/files/sync/:deviceFileId. Triggers: upload, auth:login/verify/import-session, 45s interval, manual POST /api/files/sync-now (+ GET /api/files/status diagnostics). POST /api/upload on desktop is now LOCAL-FIRST: stores the blob locally, creates a LOCAL_ONLY FileAsset row, returns a local URL (preview renders instantly, works OFFLINE), never blocks on the cloud; GET /api/upload/file/* serves the local store (organized + legacy shapes); DELETE removes locally and replays the tombstone. The v2 record sync engine is untouched — blobs travel their own endpoints.
- FILE URL NORMALIZATION: new lib/file-url.ts — any desktop-local (127.0.0.1:3080) or relative /api/upload/file/ URL entering the cloud (register avatarUrl, user profile PATCH, agency profile PATCH/PUT, and EVERY /api/sync/push mutation.data via a pre-intake pass) is rewritten to the cloud's public URL with the path suffix preserved byte-exact (the file-sync push preserves that path, so the normalized URL becomes live the moment the blob lands). Added cloud PUT /api/agency/profile (sharing the PATCH handler) because desktop outbox legacy rows replay as PUT and previously 404'd; logoUrl/coverUrl/workingDays now accepted there.
- VERIFIED BONUS FIX (real auth bug found while testing): verifySessionToken's stale-role check compared the second-resolution JWT `iat` (×1000) against the millisecond-precision `lastRoleChangeAt` — a session token issued in the SAME second as the role change (exactly register → verify) was rejected as stale up to 999ms → every fresh verification 401'd "Authentication required". Now compares in seconds on both sides (auth.ts). This also explains "authentication required"-style failures right after OTP confirm.
- i18n: added workingDays/workingDaysDesc/servicesStepDesc/serviceNameRequired to ar/en/fr.
- Verification — cloud curl matrix (/home/z/task15-tests/test15-cloud.sh): **19/19 PASS** (organized upload+path fields, byte-exact GET, register with local avatarUrl → normalized to cloud URL, dev SMS code echoed as 123456, verify → valid session, authenticated receipt upload, files/sync push + idempotent replay, pull metadata+cursor, blob byte-exact, agency create with workingDays+2 services atomically, working-days PATCH, tombstone delete).
- Verification — desktop chain integration test (test15-desktop-chain.js, real startLocalApi on :3081 + temp DB/files + stub cloud): **25/25 PASS** — schema v4 stamped on fresh DB (workingDays + FileAsset present), local-first upload → local URL + LOCAL_ONLY row, byte-exact local GET, verify → file-sync auto-pushed the avatar to the cloud (multipart + deviceFileId) and flipped the row SYNCED, /api/agencies proxied (201, workingDays+services forwarded, session token attached — the exact flow that 404'd "Not found"), DELETE → local removal + cloud tombstone + row cleanup, v3→v4 upgrade path on a downgraded DB (workingDays + FileAsset restored, data preserved).
- Verification — browser E2E (agent-browser, gateway-quirk fetch patch as in prior rounds): 12 people avatars + preset preview; REAL upload round-trip (POST 201) with the preview rendering the final cloud URL (naturalWidth>0 = visibly loaded); full register as AGENCY_OWNER → OTP (email 12345678, phone 123456 filled the 6-box input) → verified; agency wizard: hours + Arabic weekday chips (toggle verified) + services rows (2 added, prefixes A/B) + preview showing hours/days/services; create → **201** → agency dashboard loaded with the uploaded avatar in the header; profile page renders "أيام العمل: Sun · Mon · Tue · Wed · Thu · Fri" beside the hours (direct API probe confirms workingDays "0,1,2,3,4,5" returned). No page errors; eslint clean; tsc: 201 pre-existing api errors (unchanged baseline), web errors all pre-existing (none in touched files); local API node --check OK.
- Docs: DEPLOYMENT-GUIDE.md gained a "File Storage & Filing System (Round 15)" section (layout diagram, BLASTI_STORAGE_DIR / BLASTI_PUBLIC_BASE_URL / BLASTI_LOCAL_FILES_DIR env rows, desktop offline-files explanation).
- Probe accounts/agencies: probe15_*, p15* test rows in the dev cloud DB; test artifacts in /home/z/task15-tests/.

Stage Summary:
- The register avatar preview is fixed at BOTH levels: uploads are local-first (cannot fail offline; preview always renders from 127.0.0.1:3080) and the error path no longer clears the preview. Cloud URL normalization keeps every other device working once the file-sync lands the blob.
- The dev SMS OTP is now 123456 — six digits, filling all six input boxes exactly like a real code.
- Creating an agency on the desktop works again (cloud proxy + sync kick) — "Not found" is gone; working days (beside the hours) and a services step are part of the same atomic create.
- The filing system is unified: one organized bucket/year/month layout on the cloud (BLASTI_STORAGE_DIR) and the same layout on every desktop (userData/blasti-files), a FileAsset registry on both sides, safe machine filenames, legacy-layout read fallbacks, app binaries out of tmpdir, and a desktop file-sync worker (push/pull/tombstone) that makes file storage offline-first as part of the sync engine — without touching the v2 record engine.
- Next: Windows checkout should pull these files; first desktop boot runs migration v4 non-destructively (workingDays + FileAsset appear, data preserved). Queued: Session 11 provider/verification UI remainder, VPS adapter excision.

Task ID: 16
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: Fix the no-agency onboarding deadlock exposed by the Windows field log (launch gate stuck FATAL with `initialization NOT_INITIALIZED`, cloud sync/pull 403 "You do not have access to this agency", 17 local endpoints 403, `/api/agency/announcements` 500 on null agencyId, cloud socket `db.user.update` P2025 spam) so a registered AGENCY_OWNER without an agency can actually reach the agency-creation wizard and initialize the workspace afterwards.

Work Log:
- Root-caused the field log: the launch gate's `isReady === false` branch treated "session present + no agency + cloud up" as a real initialization failure (FATAL) — the agency-creation wizard inside the app was unreachable forever. The diagnostics sync probe also POSTed a fake `agencyId: 'diagnostics-probe'` which produced the misleading 403, and the stale snapshot token (issued BEFORE agency creation, `agencyId: ''`) kept the sync engine (`ensureWorkspaceInitialized`) skipping with `no-agency` even after an agency existed.
- loading-screen.js: (1) probe body now sends the real agencyId or NONE (a 400 "agencyId required" proves route+token; only 401/403-with-agencyId count as auth rejection); (2) new `noAgencyOnboarding` branch passes the gate for an authenticated non-CUSTOMER without an agency — initial-sync AND verify steps report expected onboarding instead of FATAL; (3) new step 3.1b startup SESSION REFRESH via POST /api/auth/refresh-session — adopts a re-issued token+user into blasti-auth.json + local re-import + gate context (heals agencies created elsewhere / role changes), and surfaces an honest Arabic "أعد تسجيل الدخول" warning on 401 ghost tokens.
- Cloud apps/api/src/routes/auth.ts: NEW `POST /api/auth/refresh-session` — re-issues the session token from the DB's CURRENT state (user row must exist → 401 for ghost tokens; resolves agencyId from active AgencyStaff then owned Agency, mirroring sync.ts resolveTargetAgencyId) and returns { success, token, user }.
- Cloud apps/api/src/routes/agencies.ts: POST /api/agencies 201 now returns an upgraded session token (`createSessionToken({...user, agencyId: agency.id})`) so the client can adopt the agency-aware JWT in the same round-trip; auto-derived customCode (first 3 letters of the name) is now collision-probed (base2..base50 + base36 time tail) instead of 500ing on P2002, and the create is wrapped so any P2002 race surfaces as the client-handled 409 "Agency code already taken".
- Cloud apps/api/src/index.ts: socket connect/disconnect presence writes switched `db.user.update` → `updateMany` — ghost/offline-issued tokens no longer spam P2025 on every connect/disconnect.
- Desktop local-api/index.js: (1) `/api/agency/announcements` returns `[]` for a null agencyId (was Prisma validation 500); (2) the `/api/agencies` proxy adopts the created agency into the local session immediately (`sessionUser.agencyId = data.agency.id` + `auth:login` emit) so local agency endpoints stop 403ing before the renderer's IPC lands; (3) NEW `POST /api/auth/refresh-session` proxy — forwards the stored cloud token, imports the refreshed token+user into the local session (same contract as import-session), schedules a file-sync round, passes the cloud payload through to the renderer.
- Desktop local-api/sync-service.js: `ensureWorkspaceInitialized` no longer dead-ends on a missing agencyId — it now asks the cloud to re-issue the session (`_refreshSessionFromCloud`, rate-limited 60s, single-flight preserved) and adopts the returned token+user+agencyId before resuming initialization; only a cloud-confirmed "no agency" still skips.
- Renderer create-agency-form.tsx: on create success the store now adopts the refreshed token (`setUser` FIRST, then `setSessionToken(data.token)`) — setSessionToken pushes the agency-aware token to the Electron main process (`cloud-sync:set-auth` → engine setAuth → post-auth initialization) and the local API (`local-api:set-session`), and persists it for the next launch.

Stage Summary:
- VERIFIED cloud chain (curl, /home/z/task16-tests/test16-cloud.sh): 12/12 PASS — pre-agency token has agencyId '', refresh-session honest pre-agency, pull without agencyId → 400 (not misleading 403), agency create 201 + refreshed token embedding the agency + atomic services (prefix A), OLD token heals via refresh-session (agencyId resolved from DB) and old-token pull resolves the owned agency from DB.
- VERIFIED browser E2E (agent-browser + in-page gateway fetch patch, sandbox-only artifact): fresh AGENCY_OWNER registration → OTP (email 12345678 / phone 123456) → verified → onboarding → "#/agency" shows "أنشئ مؤسستك" (CreateAgencyForm) → wizard with hours + weekday chips (الأحد…السبت) beside time inputs + services step (A استشارة, B تحليلات) → review shows hours/days/services → create → 201 → dashboard loaded; persisted store after creation: userAgencyId == tokenAgency == cmu9nmyyl… (token adoption confirmed); reload simulation restores the full dashboard (مرحباً بعودتك, Probe 👋 — queue controls, counters, stats). Screenshot: /home/z/task16-tests/16-dashboard.png.

Task ID: 26
Agent: main
Task: Fix agency creation 500 (FK violation) + fake "offline" retry error, web SyncEngine "synchronize is not a function", and Windows orphaned web dev server on port 3000 after Ctrl+C

Work Log:
- Diagnosed POST /api/agencies 500 from user's Windows log: Prisma P2003 FK violation at apps/api/src/routes/agencies.ts agency.create — the ONLY parent FK on that create is ownerId → User.id, proving the cloud DB (data/custom.db) no longer contains the token's user (cmu9hvio50000tc605dvneq4i) — a ghost session (DB reset while the stored JWT survived; matches refresh-session 401 + "cloud does not know this account" diagnostics).
- Root cause in apps/api/src/lib/auth.ts verifySessionToken: when checkStaleRole looked up the user and found none, the stale-role check was skipped entirely and the ghost token passed EVERY route.
- Fixed verifySessionToken: ghost JWT (valid signature, missing user row) now returns null → requireAuth/requireRole respond 401 "Authentication required" instead of letting writes blow up with raw FK 500s.
- Added defense in depth in apps/api/src/routes/agencies.ts POST /: explicit ownerExists pre-check returning 401 code=SESSION_USER_MISSING ("Your session refers to an account that no longer exists. Please log in again."), plus P2003 catch mapping to the same 401.
- Traced the second-click error "You are offline and this operation cannot be queued": the first click's 500 (cloud) + LAN-proxy 500 (local /api/agencies is a cloud forwarder, so it forwarded the same FK error) triggered markBothUnreachable() for 30s → ApiClient offline-mutation branch threw the misleading offline error for the non-queueable /api/agencies POST.
- Fixed api-client.ts offline-mutation branch: when navigator.onLine is true, the error now says the API recently returned a server error (honest) instead of "You are offline".
- Fixed apps/web/src/components/agency/create-agency-form.tsx handleCreate: 401 / SESSION_USER_MISSING / "no longer exists" responses now toast the new sessionInvalidRelogin i18n key and call store logout() → clean routing to the login screen instead of a dead-end toast loop.
- Added sessionInvalidRelogin key to i18n en.ts ("Your session is no longer valid — please log in again"), fr.ts, ar.ts (انتهت صلاحية جلستك — يرجى تسجيل الدخول مرة أخرى).
- Fixed apps/web/src/db/sync.ts "SyncEngine] Sync failed: synchronize is not a function": TWO bugs — (1) synchronize is exported from @nozbe/watermelondb/sync only (verified 0.28.0 main entry does not re-export it), (2) the call used the legacy two-arg synchronize(database, {...}) shape while 0.28 takes a single SyncArgs object. Both fixed.
- Windows port-3000 orphan: next dev (Next 15/16) runs the server in a child process; Ctrl+C through bun run --filter kills the bun wrapper/shim but not the whole next tree, so next-server survives holding port 3000.
- Created scripts/dev-web.mjs wrapper: (1) pre-start self-healing — detects port listeners via netstat/lsof, verifies the PID is a JS dev process (tasklist/ps) before killing, taskkill /T /F the tree; (2) spawns next via Node directly (skipping bun→cmd shim signal loss); (3) SIGINT/SIGTERM/SIGHUP/exit handlers kill the entire child tree.
- Root package.json: dev:web now runs the wrapper (dev:web:legacy keeps the old path); dev/dev:all/electron:dev:full inherit it automatically.
- Verified: eslint clean on all changed web files; tsc clean on changed API files (pre-existing errors elsewhere untouched); node --check + live smoke test of dev-web.mjs (spawns, binds, exits cleanly, sandbox preview server untouched).

Stage Summary:
- Agency creation now degrades gracefully: a ghost/invalid session produces an actionable 401 → the form clears the dead session and routes to login (en/fr/ar), and after re-login the create succeeds; valid sessions create normally.
- The misleading "You are offline and this operation cannot be queued" retry error can no longer fire while the machine is online.
- Web SyncEngine WatermelonDB sync now actually runs (import path + call shape fixed) instead of failing every cycle.
- bun run dev:web is now self-healing on Windows: orphaned next-server processes are killed on Ctrl+C AND automatically cleaned up before the next start; manual `taskkill /F /PID <pid>` / `npx kill-port 3000` no longer needed.
- USER ACTION REQUIRED: re-login once (the stored nobleman820 token points to an account that no longer exists in the cloud custom.db — register again if the credentials no longer match), then the agency setup wizard will create the agency successfully.

Task ID: 30
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: Fix "creating agency is failing and I got logout when I click create agency after filling the form" (user's Windows PC, browser at localhost:3000, dev:web + dev:api + desktop app running).

Work Log:
- Traced the failure path: create-agency-form.tsx handleCreate() → apiFetch POST /api/agencies → cloud :3003 → 401 → the ghost-session guard (res.status === 401 || SESSION_USER_MISSING) called logout() AFTER the user had filled the entire wizard, destroying all input.
- Root cause identified and reproduced with curl: the browser holds TWO credentials — the httpOnly session cookie AND the zustand-persisted sessionToken sent as Authorization: Bearer (buildAuthHeaders reads localStorage['blasti-app'].state.sessionToken on EVERY request). The cloud API's extractTokenFromRequest reads the Authorization header FIRST, so a STALE Bearer token poisons every apiClient request even when the cookie is still perfectly valid. The stale Bearer appears whenever the cloud DB no longer contains the user it names (DB reset/replaced while a browser session survived — e.g. repo pulls, db:push data loss, db-file swaps) — exactly the user's environment (their PC also had the desktop app running; the SyncEngine "LAN server has no /api/sync/pull route" + repeated "Authentication required" lines in their log confirm a dead session vs the cloud DB).
- Live proof (sandbox API): ghost-Bearer POST /api/agencies → 401; ghost-Bearer + VALID cookie → still 401 (Bearer precedence, poisoning confirmed); cookie-only POST /api/auth/refresh-session → 200 + fresh token + user. lastRoleChangeAt stale-JWT path ruled out (never written anywhere).
- NEW apps/web/src/lib/session-heal.ts: healSessionFromCookie() = cookie-ONLY POST /api/auth/refresh-session via buildCloudUrl (no Bearer) → returns fresh token+user or null (Electron/network/401/404 → null, never destructive); applyHealedSession() = gentle useAppStore.setState({user, sessionToken}) that does NOT touch currentView (setUser would yank the user out of the wizard mid-retry).
- create-agency-form.tsx: (1) NEW mount-time session validation — /api/auth/session (skipped in Electron) → if no user → heal → adopt-or-toast+logout BEFORE the user fills anything (network failures never logout); (2) submit-time ghost guard upgraded: on 401/SESSION_USER_MISSING → heal → adopt fresh token → RETRY the create once → only if the heal fails → toast + logout. All input is preserved in the healed path.
- auth-provider.tsx: boot settle made BLOCKING on web — after persist rehydration and BEFORE rendering any authed UI: /api/auth/session (maxRetries 0, 6s AbortSignal.timeout cap) → {} means invalid → cookie-heal → adopt (silent) or toast+logout; network failure → proceed without logout (offline tolerance). This closes the boot race where eagerly-mounted screens 401'd with the ghost Bearer and handleAuthExpired logged out before the heal finished. Electron branch unchanged in behavior: IPC + import-session restore, no cloud check, then render.
- fetch-with-retry.ts handleAuthExpired: web branch now tries the cookie heal before store.logout() — any mid-flight 401 anywhere in the app self-heals instead of destroying the session.
- Rate-limiter notes during testing: register 3/min/IP, auth-verify 5/15min/IP, login 10/15min/IP — restarted the API (daemon-start) twice to clear the in-memory buckets.
- Browser E2E (agent-browser), 4 scenarios: T1 golden path (register healfixowner1 → OTP 12345678/123456 → wizard HEALFIX1 + service row → POST 201 → welcome wizard → dashboard) ✓. T2 THE USER'S BUG: filled whole wizard as healfixowner2, poisoned the stored token with a ghost JWT mid-session → submit → console shows 401 → "[CreateAgencyForm] 401 on create — session healed from cookie, retrying create" → retry 201 → dashboard, NO logout, work preserved ✓. T3 fully dead session (ghost token + cleared cookies) at boot → clean logout to landing with toast ✓. T4 ghost token + valid cookie at reload → "[AuthProvider] Session reported invalid but cookie healed it — fresh token adopted" → dashboard intact; store verified: isAuthenticated=true, tokenIsGhost=false, fresh 356-char token, view=agency-dashboard ✓.
- bun run lint clean; web :3000 + API :3003 healthy after changes.

Stage Summary:
- Creating an agency no longer logs the user out: a stale/ghost Bearer token is self-healed from the still-valid httpOnly cookie and the create is transparently retried — the user keeps their filled wizard and lands on the dashboard. A truly dead session is now detected EARLY (app boot via blocking settle + wizard mount) with a clear "session expired, please log in again" message instead of a post-form-fill nuke; dead-at-boot also logs out cleanly before any input is wasted.
- The root cause on the user's PC is a browser session (Bearer) that predates their current cloud DB. With the fix the app heals it automatically (if the cookie is valid) or cleanly forces re-login BEFORE the wizard — after re-login (or re-registration) agency creation works. NOTE for the user: if login itself says wrong credentials, their old account is not in the current DB file — just register again; the app no longer punishes them mid-form either way.
- Electron/desktop untouched: session-heal and the wizard mount check no-op in Electron contexts; the desktop keeps its local-first session machinery (IPC setLocalApiSession + import-session). Desktop still benefits from the AuthProvider reorganization (same restore logic, now before render).
- Test artifacts: ghost JWT /tmp/ghost-token.ts(.txt); probe data: healfix1751 (customer), healfixowner1 + agency "Heal Fix Agency" (HEALFIX1), healfixowner2 + agency "Ghost Heal Test Agency" (GHOSTH1).

Task ID: 31-c
Agent: Explore (desktop local API research)
Task: Research bugs 7, 8, 12, 11 (staff creation userId, staff-branch, password reset, subscription plans in desktop)

Work Log:
- Read worklog Tasks 27-30 for context: desktop web UI = same Next.js app; in Electron ALL apiFetch/apiClient calls go to the embedded local API http://127.0.0.1:3080 (apps/web/src/lib/api-client.ts:157 ELECTRON_LOCAL_API_BASE, getApiBaseUrl:173-191) — every reported desktop failure is a local-API contract/missing-route problem, not a web bug.
- Grep "userId is required" repo-wide → single hit at apps/desktop/local-api/index.js:5716 inside POST /api/agency/staff/create (5710-5739).
- Traced the full staff-creation flow: agency-employees.tsx handleCreate (177-218) POSTs { agencyId, username, fullName, password, role } to /api/agency/staff/create (NO userId — the UI creates a NEW user account); cloud POST /api/agency/staff/create (apps/api/src/routes/agency.ts:2665-2760) validates via createStaffSchema+agencyId (validations.ts:142-148), checks verifyAgencyOwnership (2682-2688), hashes password cloud-side (2714, scrypt salt:hash per apps/api/src/lib/password.ts:12-26), CREATES the User row (2717-2727) then the AgencyStaff link (2730-2741), returns { staff, initialPassword } 201. Local route instead expects { userId, role, permissions, isActive } (5715) — an old "link existing user" contract the UI never calls → body lacks userId → 400 "userId is required" on desktop only (cloud accepts the same body fine).
- Checked the outbox/replay machinery before proposing a fix: logPendingMutation (index.js:667-693), withOutboxTransaction (751-756), sync-service replay re-calls the SAME cloud path (sync-service.js:1392-1543, requestUrl = cloudBaseUrl + mutation.path at 1463-1466). Replaying a local staff/create would make the CLOUD create a second user with a different id → AgencyStaff.userId divergence → pull dependency failures. So user creation must stay cloud-native (same decision as Round-15 agency creation, forwardAgencies proxy at index.js:1885-1950).
- BUG 8: grep'd both schemas — cloud Prisma AgencyStaff.branchId String? (packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma:443, relation 459); local SQLite AgencyStaff."branchId" TEXT + FK→Branch (apps/desktop/local-api/lib/schema-init-sql.js:253, 267). User model has NO branchId anywhere. End-to-end gap: createStaffSchema has no branchId (validations.ts:142-148); cloud POST /staff/create doesn't accept/set it; cloud PATCH /staff/:id (2765-2889) doesn't either; local staff routes identical; UI create dialog (agency-employees.tsx:587-673) has only username/fullName/password/role — no branch selector (only branch reference in the file is the canManageBranches permission at line 58).
- BUG 12: enumerated every web password flow: login-form.tsx:246 POST /api/auth/forgot-password, :268 POST /api/auth/reset-password; desktop-agency-login.tsx:308 POST /api/auth/forgot-password (this is the desktop login shell, apps/web/src/app/page.tsx:107); agency sidebar change-password adaptive-sidebar.tsx:146 PATCH /api/user/change-password; customer change-password customer-settings.tsx:165 + customer/profile/use-profile-data.ts:161 + customer-profile.tsx:259 (same endpoint); admin-users.tsx:204 POST /api/admin/users/:id/reset-password. Cloud has all four (auth.ts:988, auth.ts:1061, user.ts:136, admin.ts:2055). Local API has NONE (grep "password" in index.js shows only login/register/verify/device-credential) → every one hits app.notFound (index.js:6075-6078) → 404 { success:false, error:'Not found', path } — exactly the reported desktop "Not found".
- BUG 11: agency-subscription.tsx fetches GET /api/agency/subscription?agencyId=… (2082-2096) and renders plans from data.availablePlans (comment at 103; consumption at 2208, 2268-2375; empty list renders "No plans available right now" at 2918-2925 — silent, no error toast). Cloud GET /subscription embeds availablePlans via db.subscriptionPlan.findMany (agency.ts:3373-3475, query 3393-3403). Local GET /api/agency/subscription (index.js:4639-4713) hardcodes availablePlans: [] in ALL three return paths (4651, 4675, 4706); GET /api/agency/subscription-plans (5047-5056) returns { plans: [] } with a STALE comment "SubscriptionPlan table is NOT synced locally". Verified that comment is wrong: lib/sync-registry.json:24-25 lists SubscriptionPlan+PlanFeature as synced (syncOrder 2/3, CLOUD_AUTHORITATIVE), sync-helpers.ts:171-178 treats them as GLOBAL feed models, and both tables exist in schema-init-sql.js:111-159. Cloud plans are seeded by packages/db/prisma/seed.ts:64-122 (FREE/BASIC/PREMIUM) + admin enterprise plans; they reach the local DB purely via the sync pull — no local seeding needed. Local db is a REAL Prisma client over SQLite (lib/db.js) so the cloud query can be mirrored verbatim (include: { features: true } works — local routes already use include, e.g. 3188-3196).
- Built the COMPLETE local route table (method+path, 6316-line file) and the cloud password/staff/plans/branches subset for the report; noted missing local business routes beyond the 4 bugs (subscription pay/cancel/unsubscribe, PATCH branch, faqs) as gaps at a glance.
- Read-only research: no source files modified; only this worklog entry appended.

Stage Summary:
- BUG 7 ROOT CAUSE: local POST /api/agency/staff/create (apps/desktop/local-api/index.js:5710-5739) implements a dead "link existing user by userId" contract and rejects at 5716, while the web UI (agency-employees.tsx:188-197) and the cloud route (agency.ts:2665-2760) both speak the "create new user from username/fullName/password/role" contract — desktop-only failure because in Electron that POST lands on :3080. FIX: replace the local handler with a cloud-forwarding proxy (pattern: forwardAgencies index.js:1885-1950) that passes the body through with the session Bearer, returns the cloud { staff, initialPassword } 201, kicks fileSync.schedule + syncService.triggerSyncNow on success so User (USER_SYNC projection) + AgencyStaff pull down, and returns 503 CLOUD_UNREACHABLE offline. Do NOT implement local user creation and do NOT logPendingMutation the create body (replay would create a divergent second cloud user).
- BUG 8 ROOT CAUSE: the "staff belongs to branch" feature is schema-only: AgencyStaff.branchId exists in BOTH prisma (schema.prisma:443) and local SQLite (schema-init-sql.js:253) but no API accepts it (validations.ts:142-148, cloud staff/create 2665+, local equivalents) and the create dialog (agency-employees.tsx:587-673) has no branch selector. FIX: add optional branchId — Select in the create dialog fed by GET /api/agency/branches (local 2820 / cloud agency.ts:389) → extend the staff/create schema at agency.ts:2668-2671 with branchId + validate it belongs to the agency → set it on the AgencyStaff create (2730-2735); local proxy passes it through transparently; optionally accept it in PATCH /staff/:id. Sync already carries the column (AgencyStaff synced VERSION_AWARE, syncOrder 7).
- BUG 12 ROOT CAUSE: zero password routes exist in the local API; forgot/reset/change-password all 404 via the notFound handler (index.js:6075-6078) with the literal "Not found". Password reset is cloud-native (reset tokens live in cloud memory auth.ts:1024-1032; messages sent cloud-side). FIX: add three thin cloud proxies mirroring forwardAppVersions (index.js:1820-1873): POST /api/auth/forgot-password, POST /api/auth/reset-password (no auth needed), PATCH /api/user/change-password (forward Bearer). GOTCHA: after a cloud-side change-password the local LocalDeviceCredential verifier still matches the OLD password for offline unlock (stored at login, index.js:239) — either delete the credential for sessionUser.id on success or document it. Admin reset-password (admin.ts:2055) intentionally NOT proxied — desktop has no admin API by design (index.js:15).
- BUG 11 ROOT CAUSE: local GET /api/agency/subscription hardcodes availablePlans: [] (index.js:4706 etc.) and GET /api/agency/subscription-plans hardcodes { plans: [] } (5051) behind a stale "not synced" comment; the plans ARE in the local DB via the global sync feed (registry 24-25, GLOBAL_MODELS sync-helpers.ts:176-177, tables 111-159), so the UI silently renders an empty catalog. FIX: mirror the cloud query (agency.ts:3393-3403) with db.subscriptionPlan.findMany({ where: { isActive: true, OR: [{ isEnterprise:false }, ...(agencyId?[{ isEnterprise:true, ownerAgencyId:agencyId }]:[])] }, include:{ features:true }, orderBy:{ sortOrder:'asc' } }) in BOTH local routes, and populate recentTransactions from db.transaction (synced APPEND_ONLY). No local seeding required.
- Local API pattern gotchas for implementers: authMiddleware = requireAuth() (index.js:326-376, Bearer/X-Local-Token/?token vs in-memory sessionToken, sets c.set('user', sessionUser)); agency scoping via sessionUser.agencyId or resolveSessionAgencyId(?agencyId= with ownership check) (index.js:125-141); business writes go through withOutboxTransaction + logPendingMutation/logDeterministicOutcome (751-756, 667-734); response envelopes vary (legacy {success,data}, cloud-shaped {staff}, raw rows) — match the cloud contract per route; cloud base = cloudBaseUrl() = BLASTI_CLOUD_URL || http://localhost:3003 (182-184); 404 handler logs registered-route count → after adding routes a FULL desktop restart is required (6076).
- At-a-glance gaps seen while building the route table (beyond the 4 bugs): no local POST /api/agency/subscription/pay, /subscription/cancel, /subscription/unsubscribe (subscription purchase from desktop will 404 next); no GET /api/faqs (subscription FAQs silently empty); local POST /api/agency/staff replay body lacks agencyId and the replay shim only patches /api/reservations paths (sync-service.js:1443-1456) — legacy rows will fail cloud replay.

GOTCHA for follow-up: cloud file-push re-dates the storage folder by push time (storage.ts:123-125) — a blob pushed across a month boundary lands under a different yyyy/mm than the preserved URL suffix (file-url.ts:24) → 404; normalize from the pushed FileAsset.storagePath instead.

After fixes: fully restart the desktop app (stale local-api build keeps 404ing) and clear `permanent_failed` rows via Settings → Sync recovery / re-save profile.

- BUG 6 — GET /api/agency/branches (~2833): mirrored cloud query `include: { _count: { select: { counters: true, staff: true } } }, orderBy: [{ isMain: 'desc' }, { name: 'asc' }]` and returned dual-key `{ success, branches, data }` (pattern from the staff route). POST /api/agency/branches (~2856): now destructures+persists nameAr/nameFr/isMain, runs the "unset other mains" updateMany sweep INSIDE the same withOutboxTransaction (same as PATCH route), kept the logDeterministicOutcome outbox row, and returns `{ success, branch, data }` 201 to match cloud. Branch-counters GET (~3178): dual-key `{ success, counters, data }` — plus a latent-bug fix (see deviation below).
- BUG 7 — POST /api/agency/staff/create (~5748) rewritten as a cloud-forwarding proxy (forwardAgencies pattern): raw body forwarded verbatim with `Authorization: Bearer ${sessionToken}` to `${cloudBaseUrl()}/api/agency/staff/create`; cloud status+body passed through verbatim ({ staff, initialPassword } 201); on 2xx kicks `fileSync.schedule('staff-created')` + `require('./sync-service').triggerSyncNow()` so the new User+AgencyStaff pull down; on fetch failure 503 CLOUD_UNREACHABLE with the "Creating a staff account requires an internet connection…" message. After a 2xx change-password the local device credential is revoked via `db.localDeviceCredential.deleteMany({ where: { userId: sessionUser.id } })` so offline unlock can't keep matching the OLD password (next login re-stores a fresh credential).

- ROOT CAUSE (two stacked holes, both closed):
